Mixed language, cache and history qualification

P10d2b1 · 2026-10-11T16:47:16.058Z · v22.12.0 · linux/x64 · AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics. 5 alternating fresh processes per implementation and size.

Superseded unbundled bootstrap: Small cold indexing missed the ten-percent guard (+11.5%). All thirty fresh observations, all sizes and every graph/history identity are preserved. This source hash records the earlier TypeScript-loader worker; it differs from the plain JavaScript bundle implementation. Final parser qualification · Final mixed comparison.

Local cold/warm/history/tree-memory comparison: NEEDS WORK
Ten-percent limits against the archived baseline implementation on identical authored mixed workloads. Exact full graph digests hold across cold/population/warm, edited dependency replay versus uncached source, every baseline/current run, and all four stored historical snapshots. Every first repetition also compares two history jobs with an independent single-job run. History resume skips all four completed snapshots.

The monorepo combines all eighteen Tree-sitter structural adapters, PHP, TypeScript and original Vue/Svelte/Astro sources; Express/Nest, FastAPI/Flask/Django, Rails/Laravel, eight Go router profiles, Java/Kotlin MVC/WebFlux and ASP.NET 8/9/10, and six Rust router/version profiles. Recorded target inputs qualify source contracts on Linux; they do not establish native Windows/macOS execution or framework runtime validity. Target dependencies, toolchains, applications, plugins and hooks never run. The literal fixtures are authored qualification inputs, not upstream repository accuracy evidence.

Cold scans have no cache. Population, warm and a shared Node dependency edit are timed separately. Both original consumer call targets bind to the shared source; the edit invalidates their connected cache unit while unrelated TypeScript units stay hits. Warm graph, syntax and semantic cache events are recorded by analyzer; a qualified PHP graph hit skips native-tree hydration. The cache keeps at most two versions per unit, 256 MiB compressed overall and 64 MiB decoded per entry; oversized/corrupt entries fall back to source rather than blocking indexing.

The four-commit history edits the shared dependency and an original PHP body, then renames a Vue component. Two history workers materialize original Git trees; snapshots include complete entities, relations and diagnostics, not just counts. Only working-tree Git metrics and Git-availability diagnostics are normalized for live-versus-revision comparisons. All semantic metadata, evidence and original ranges remain in the digest.

A separate process polls every thread's descendant list every 25 ms, with an acknowledged start marker before synchronous work. RSS sums include history and parser children; shared pages can be counted repeatedly, exiting processes can make a poll incomplete, and peaks between polls can be missed. Raw parent VmHWM is cumulative since exec, not an isolated warm/history peak. Native OS readers, broader WASM/in-memory budgets, deeper histories and production-repository accuracy remain additional P10d gates. This report records its named phase against the archived baseline.

Baseline: 055e6d2fde933f14a295888b251d569d72db5f48. Frozen current source: a9cc966d1ecf8002f6da9dca440745c3c09f7db90a02b6f28d949918e5d4d7f9 (321 files). Reproduce: node --import tsx scripts/benchmark-mixed-compatibility.mjs --baseline-ref 055e6d2fde933f14a295888b251d569d72db5f48 --repetitions 5 --phase P10d2b1 --output docs/parser-lifetime-mixed-performance.html. Roadmap.
